# FS25 vault capability audit

Status: **in progress**, last updated 6 November 2026. This audit reopens the question that the 65-entry companion validation matrix could not answer: whether every vault mod with useful, distinct data has an honest Farm Dashboard reading. The completed matrix is evidence for its tested integrations, not a vault-wide pass.

## Inventory boundary

- Current read-only `P:\FS25 MOD VAULT\by-category` enumeration: **8,012 ZIP paths**. The maintained vault index dated 26 October lists 8,011, so it is not an exact current inventory.
- The 23 September source-screening inventory covered **665 ZIP names**. By filename, 602 remain in the current category tree, 63 are absent, and 7,407 distinct current names were outside that older sample. Filename overlap is a triage aid, not proof of identical bytes or versions.
- The read-only `2026-10-31-scan-vault.py` scan covered all 8,012 current category-tree ZIP paths. **1,803** contain Lua and **1,549** declare source files in `modDesc.xml`. **1,201** Lua-bearing ZIP names were outside the older 665-name screen. The full machine-readable result is `Z:\Graham\agent-comms\farmhub-companion-acceptance\VAULT-SCAN-2026-10-31.json`.
- `FS25_Bale_Storage.zip` in `uncategorized` was unreadable as a ZIP; its contents and playability are unproved. No vault files were modified.
- A title/category/source-file keyword pass put **118 ZIPs across 91 normalized titles** into an initial review queue. The queue includes duplicate versions, RF packages, native-data overlap and false positives; **118 is not a missing-integration count**. The sortable 1,201-row Lua triage file is `Z:\Graham\agent-comms\farmhub-companion-acceptance\VAULT-LUA-TRIAGE-2026-10-31.csv`. Every row is still a source-review lead, not an acceptance result. Lower-scoring rows must also be assessed before claiming the vault audit exhaustive.

## Decision rule

For each candidate, establish the exact current ZIP hash/version and source-owned state; decide whether that state is distinct from the normal game export, farm-scoped and safely readable; add a bounded collector and visible reading where justified; then compare a fresh nonempty game state, committed export and installed page. Classify native-game overlap and detection-only mods honestly. Never call an empty, unavailable or settings-only result a gameplay pass. RF-owned Ally work has priority; no direct Graham-PC UI use, public release, main push or RF/VPS changes are in scope.

This list mixes accepted bounded readings, source-tested readers and unresolved leads. Each row's status states its evidence limit; the table is not a vault-wide pass. Further source checks may add, remove or reprioritise candidates.

Implement Usage Counter private candidate: V5 5.2.27 installer SHA-256 `D74799CCB87A4F94C14156E61A8404397E80251086496310216D3B36304BB087`, exporter 5.1.0.20 ZIP SHA-256 `20C52FF73A9CF20B41ED9790C3E8F58D0AA4193C88A2A254F2B5AEEE4DE02662`, and the exact v1.2.0.0 vault ZIP SHA-256 `B7916A6407795B7A3DE1D1175F0FD455B6A7E2014D7355339859DB33E8DDE4F2` are staged under `Z:\Graham\agent-comms\farmhub-companion-acceptance\implement-usage-candidate`. The exporter archive has 98 safe, unique entries and a matching runtime/descriptor version. Focused collector and merge tests, Vite build, typecheck and locale verification passed. The frozen V3 packet SHA-256 `F5DC9400AEA2C00833CC504049E14773FEB64A7A862F2430AB15C47754859CE6` returned **PARTIAL**. The installed V5 5.2.27 page, committed generation 7 export and mod-loaded game showed 13 owned implements, 14 area counters, one bale counter and zero used counters; all 15 rows were farm 1 with 0.00 ha or 0 bales. No supported implement was hitched to a controllable vehicle, so Tina could not observe the mod HUD or safely make a working pass. This proves a zero-state, owned-row/unit data path only; nonzero game/export/UI agreement, accrual, reset and hide-on-uncheck remain untested. I independently rehashed the exact packet, installer, exporter and third-party ZIP; reviewed active marker, decoded export, installed-page screenshots, load lines and restoration readback. StockGuard loaded without refusal, one Windows row and eight generations were reported; no Lua stack appeared, though separate texture/console/mission errors did. Ally was restored by baseline hashes. A zero-initialized counter or native operating hours cannot establish worked area or wrapped-bale behavior.

Bale Batch Tracker private candidate: V5 5.2.28 installer SHA-256 `8E91F490B7E892D67B9FD735C3F570E30103CC3E5F3D37E073B392D531FF9806`, exporter 5.1.0.21 ZIP SHA-256 `02E0CAD011F956D145BE18A15CC7E5602A97ED461DA026DE409741A25E536060` and exact tracker ZIP SHA-256 `C614FF07E8924D9666A731A12DD07B8F7FDEE9DA36C643275C171B16722B3EE6` are staged under `Z:\Graham\agent-comms\farmhub-companion-acceptance\bale-batch-candidate`. The exporter archive has 99 safe, unique entries with matching runtime/descriptor version. The collector accepts only a mod snapshot whose `farmId` exactly equals the export farm and never copies the mod's bale-object keys or progress-value arrays. Source test rejects a shared global and an unknown/wrong farm; UI typecheck, Vite build and 27-locale coverage passed. Frozen V4 packet SHA-256 `F47CF50C92DD7A37AC31117BA7458BB176DFF601ACD7E93580BA3505D8B10CC0` returned a bounded **PASS** for nonempty grouped bale state with normal StockGuard selected. I independently rehashed the packet and all three exact artifacts, reviewed the mod HUD and installed page screenshots, decoded export slots, active markers, log and restoration readback. The game panel and committed generations 6/7 showed 355 tracked bales (126 physical, 229 stored), 18 ready silage bales, zero fermenting and four named fill-type groups; hay/storage rows and rounded litres matched. The installed V5 page matched later generations 8/9: the same counts/states, 94,502 L ready silage and 882,700 L straw. Straw had been 882,893 L in the earlier game panel and generations 6/7; the 193 L change occurred before the later export, and no later game-panel reading was captured. Thus exact three-way straw volume at one instant is unproved, while each measured game/export and export/page pair agreed. No in-progress fermentation, foreign-farm bale, hide-on-uncheck or bale movement was tested. One Windows row, nine generations, one target/FarmDash/StockGuard load line each and no Lua stack were observed; separate texture, console and mission errors appeared. Tina reported full Ally hash restoration.
Vehicle Inspection HU V2 was privately packaged as V5 5.2.26 installer SHA-256 `C44F866FE17E9D8A233C283D814D026C43DD8CCAD8FC919C8859BBBD8C031C7A` and exporter 5.1.0.18 ZIP SHA-256 `C8CC8E83CFE406A9C7B58BB583AD253407E1EA7E53CE0D399CF9711F6F580537`, staged with the exact HU ZIP under `Z:\Graham\agent-comms\farmhub-companion-acceptance\vehicle-inspection-hu-candidate`. The bounded `FARMHUB-ALLY-20261031-VAULT-V2` packet SHA-256 `727A1A5D437FD4D368FA607904B592C172366F04CC34F905B41D9E4C9D600A76` returned a substantive **FAIL**. The HU game tab showed 41 inspection vehicles, including the farm's John Deere 6R 250 at 12 months remaining/Valid; committed exports in two runs showed only `readState=settings-only`, `reason=no-inspection-register`, and the installed full page reflected that empty export. The normal StockGuard build accepted HU and the measured logs showed no Lua stack. The original collector used `ctx.global("g_huManager")`, while the exact HU `HUMain:loadMap` writes that name in HU's own mod environment. The collector now reads the active mod environment via `ctx.symbol`, with a `HUMain.manager` fallback. A focused Fengari test reproduces a populated mod-local register and rejects a shadow shared-engine register. The subsequent V2R1 installed retest passed this corrected read path. Tina reported full Ally hash restoration after V2; no public release was made.

The corrected exporter 5.1.0.19 ZIP SHA-256 `01FE77ACA88E7314F42A956732EA5F409F725DB5012A15E02DF1781C4E96EF63` is privately staged under `Z:\Graham\agent-comms\farmhub-companion-acceptance\vehicle-inspection-hu-retest`; it has 98 ZIP members, no duplicate or unsafe names, the corrected collector, and matching `modDesc.xml` version. The unchanged V5 5.2.26 installer and exact HU ZIP were independently rehashed. Frozen packet `TEST-PACKET-V2R1.md` SHA-256 `15941098D96433046C7FDA85BBC0A5DEFB00EB0F100CF031E21A2E6E1EFA68F8` returned **PASS for the HU register reading path** with normal StockGuard selected. I independently rehashed all four artifacts, checked the committed active marker (generation 7, slot b, sequence 8), decoded export, game and installed-page screenshots, log load lines and restoration readback. The game register showed 41 vehicles across farms; the export reported 35 farm-1 vehicles and exactly 35 farm-1 rows, excluding the six Farm 100 rows. Its John Deere 6R 250 row has a 12-month interval and 12 months remaining; the installed page showed 35 in register, zero due/overdue/decommissioned, and the same 12-month reading. The measured log contains one load line each for HU, Farm Dashboard and StockGuard, ten committed generations and no Lua error. Tina's full profile/mod/save/shortcut restoration readback matched the recorded baseline. The newly registered full intervals prove the **read path**, not a month-countdown, due/overdue transition, performed inspection, custom interval or hide-on-uncheck.

The first private test candidate is V5 5.2.25 installer SHA-256 `8B36AC3A05C095541D2B5886329DD28FC159A078B98FA5EB86F702CC2BF4A67B` plus exporter mod 5.1.0.17 SHA-256 `BEB286136103AFC9F06B5F82D1DFE51D5870BBA8C9A0F6942A7EE27F3DF02703`, staged under `Z:\Graham\agent-comms\farmhub-companion-acceptance\production-control-candidate`. The mod ZIP has 96 members, no duplicate or backslash entry names, and the collector is listed in `modDesc.xml`. The bounded `FARMHUB-ALLY-20261031-VAULT-V1` packet SHA-256 `E13BD9E6B5BE0F7ED5E16C2DD7CCF9EBD1CED667C26F49168773ACB882A9A299` received a substantive result at 20:01 UTC. I rehashed the frozen packet, staged installer, staged mod ZIP and exact vault Production Control ZIP; all match their specified hashes. On a disposable unsaved save, the game displayed Small Glass Greenhouse Chili Peppers changing from 100%/528 cycles/£2 to 150%/792 cycles/£3 while six other lines remained 100%. Committed export generation 9 reported farm 1, seven lines, one changed, Chili `baseCyclesPerMonth=528`, `cyclesPerMonth=792`, `baseCostPerMonth=2.4`, `costPerMonth=3.6`, all exact 1.5 ratios; the installed full page showed the same rounded readings. Unchecking Production Control removed its load line, export page and app link. One Windows row, committed generations and no Lua stack in the measured run were reported; Tina's full hash restoration readback matched the as-found Ally baseline. This is a **StockGuard-off configured-rate pass** only: the first load with normal StockGuard stopped at `UNBOUND_ADAPTER (FS25_ProductionControl)` before any export. StockGuard is RF-owned and was not modified; a combined normal-set acceptance requires an RF-owned compatibility decision and a fresh bounded test. The chain stayed inactive, so realized throughput/cost and MP paths remain untested. A momentary app `Stale` badge was observed but no wrong page value established.

Native-overlap examples need a different decision: `Statistics Extended` v1.0.0.1 (`FS25_364343_FS25_statsExtended.zip`, SHA-256 `EE6C655FE2E61D2DC0A19B14884AC316C802F106C1729D85A31095BA2450643C`) reads `farm.stats.statistics` session and total counters and `farm.stats:getTotalValue()` achievement progress for the local farm when its in-game tab opens. Its five Lua files have no savegame XML writer or new counter accumulator; the package is a presentation of native statistics, not a distinct mod-owned ledger. `Animal Storage Overview` reads standard husbandry storage/food values to extend the in-game Animals frame. Those can motivate better generic Farm Dashboard views, but their source does not yet establish unique mod-owned state requiring an optional collector. `Ingame Time Operating Hours` changes the game's effective hour/fuel behaviour; avoid counting a second copy of native hours as a new mod-specific metric.

Two high-ranked vault leads are **already source-integrated but lack a meaningful nonempty installed pass**. Field Profitability Ledger v1.0.1.0 (`FS25_369866_FS25_FieldProfitabilityLedger.zip`, SHA-256 `F4A832326835C42D4454376250D6997427C6635A1F7030432684AB0342D80B32`) exposes its single-player `Runtime.SinglePlayerRuntime.listCycles({farmId=...})` and `queryCycle` methods. FarmHub's existing farm-filtered reader queries up to eight cycle headers and three summaries, and the catalog accurately says single-player only. The historical B2 matrix marked its reader unavailable, so a nonempty field-cycle game/export/UI comparison is still needed. The uncategorized `KM_80685_Field_Profitability_Ledger.zip` is byte-identical; the GitHub-named v1.0.0.0 archive is an older distinct version. Farmland Marketplace v1.0.5.0 (`FS25_369241_FS25_FarmlandMarketplace.zip`, SHA-256 `331588DE99894BB62FB8EC5D43B28F8B29C64CE6674C204E6A6C4C943DAF7DB1`) exposes `FlaechenVermarktung.manager.state` with sale offers, lease offers and leases. FarmHub already filters those rows to offers/contracts touching the export farm, but B2 reported unavailable; a live offer or lease on a disposable save is needed before claiming meaningful value. These are acceptance gaps, not new missing adapters.

Dependency packages must be tested together, without silently requiring unrelated mods.

Further source checks for the next candidates:

- Field To-Do List v0.1.0.17 (`GH_knoellix__FS25_ToDo_FS25_FieldToDoList.zip`, SHA-256 `D1B428B296F93906AD8A297E07726F3FE7F70C1828452909791B0B8CF798BE18`) persists manual/field tasks with text, completed state, field and optional `farmId` in save-local `fieldToDoList.xml`; planned crops are keyed only by field ID. The current `ToDoManager` exposes `manualTasks` and a farm-filtered method, but that method treats a task with **nil farmId** as belonging to whichever farm asks. A FarmHub reader must instead require an exact farm ID on every task, withhold legacy unkeyed tasks unless an independently verified migration completed, and recheck field ownership for planned crops. These are distinct plans and assignments, not native field work completed; suggestions from the mod's scanner should not be copied as user-created tasks. No collector, nonempty task, committed export or installed comparison exists. This package is separate from the already catalogued Task List mod.
- Futures Market v1.2.0.0 (`KM_82100_FUTURES_MARKET.zip`, SHA-256 `737C03BB08AAC585C7430095ECF5F47C9D31AFF825D41017B4FCF949290E5F2C`) saves open long/short commodity contracts with explicit `farmId`, crop, amount, leverage, entry price, posted margin and an expiry index to save-local `futuresMarket.xml`. Its server creates/closes positions and synchronizes the contract list to clients; closing removes the row and posts any payout to native farm money, so the current list is **open positions**, not a realized-profit ledger. A new read-only collector/full page filters the live server list to the exact export farm and reports recorded terms and total posted margin, without calling `getContractPnL`, close, update or money methods. The mod sets `expiration = currentPeriod + term` and compares it with `currentPeriod`; until an actual rollover is observed, the page deliberately labels the raw number a **mod expiry index**, not a guaranteed calendar date. Focused farm-isolation/client-withholding tests, typecheck, Vite build and 27-locale checks passed. This is source-tested only: no private package, nonempty installed game/export/page comparison, margin call or settlement pass.
- Financial Dashboard v2.0.0.0 (`KM_83516_Financial_Dashboard.zip`, SHA-256 `CAB05E086903E5838DF147EDADEE55D5CF937254413C7784B7F785C7DA5025C8`) declares `multiplayer supported="false"`. Its `FinancialDashboard` listener samples the local mission balance, saves at most 500 balance samples and up to 365 daily aggregates to `financialDashboard.xml`, and checks the saved `farmId` against the local player's farm on load. Those dated observations could add a **single-player, local-farm history** reading; they are not a server-wide account ledger. The mod calculates inflows/outflows from differences between sampled balances, explicitly calling them estimates and treating changes while the game was closed as unknown. A future reader must require single-player and exact farm identity, show sample history/estimated movements with freshness, and avoid relabelling differences as classified transactions. No collector, nonempty history or installed comparison exists. The current balance alone duplicates native finance.
- GN Realism - Factory Contracts v1.1.0.0 (`KM_82639_Factory_Contracts.zip`, SHA-256 `60BAD2025EE48F633044FF5E79C9D5F346C57CD4D42FBD501A36347576230627`) stores signed contracts in `g_currentMission.FactoryContracts.contracts`, keyed by contract ID with an explicit `farmId`, destination, product, quantity, locked price, delivery windows and status; it also retains tickets, settlements and supply orders. These are distinct trade obligations rather than native crop stock. Its manager exposes `loadState`, and the server loads the save-local XML before authoritative readings are safe. Although the descriptor declares multiplayer support, `loadMap` still comments that moving the in-memory contract/ticket register to server authority and streams/events is TODO; do not infer multiplayer parity from that descriptor. A first reader would require loaded server state, filter signed contracts to the exact export farm, avoid unsold board offers and avoid mutating validation/build methods. A bounded nonempty contract game/export/installed comparison is needed; no collector or gameplay acceptance exists here.
- Custom Used Market v1.0.0.0 has two byte-identical vault ZIPs (`FS25_374002_Custom_Used_Market.zip` and `KM_82867_Custom_Used_Market.zip`), SHA-256 `23C389144CE970463E3617EDBE06E56CFFA1137683AA3D122926ED05AF7BBB1E`. The authoritative `CustomUsedMarket.settings` contains save-wide offer-generation/discount/duration controls; `offerMonthsLeft` is a persisted per-offer remaining-month map and is copied to each generated native `VehicleSaleSystem` item as `cumMonthsLeft`. The actual vehicle, price and sale listing live in the native used-vehicle system, so duplicating those alone adds no mod-specific reading. Remaining months could add value to a clearly labelled **shared market** view, not the current farm's owned fleet; a reader would need to join only current generated offers by the mod's stable key, avoid stale tracking entries, and prove nonempty offer aging across a period. This is a distinct market-wide candidate, lower priority than farm-owned state, not a farm-scoped missing integration count.
- Base Invoices v1.2.1.0 (`KM_75762_Invoices.zip`, SHA-256 `08930335CCFDE1227AB4935BD98187C8F2D1C405B1CC341B4C13DB74A3E63BFB`) is already integrated through `InvoicesDataCollector.lua` and the app's Invoices section. Its `InvoiceRepository` saves invoice IDs, sender/recipient farm IDs, line amounts, VAT, penalty, date and state to save-local `invoices.xml`; this is distinct from native balance alone. Source review found a v1.2.1.0 `PROPOSED=5` state absent from the existing reader. The mod intentionally presents an unapproved proposal as **incoming to its issuer** and **outgoing from its requester**, whereas the reader had inferred direction from sender/recipient payment IDs and labelled state 5 as unpaid. Source now uses the manager's incoming/outgoing lists, labels proposals separately and excludes them from payable summary totals; a focused Lua proposal test, typecheck, Vite build and 27-locale verification passed. This correction is **source-tested only and is not in the already staged V15 Auto Sell Silo package**. No exact v1.2.1.0 proposed invoice has been compared across game, fresh export and installed page, and payment/penalty gameplay remains untested. The base Invoices package is one existing integration, not another missing collector.

- SIM Dashboard Interface v1.0.0.0 (`FS25_SIMDashboard_Interface.zip`, SHA-256 `208F7E62DCAC98833EBE84C7349DA6486C63ACB1D9555FFCE74C6DD4B17429E6`) describes a game-data bridge to an Android second-screen app. Its only Lua file is a single 160,811-character compressed/obfuscated line; the exact source cannot be safely classified as exposing a distinct farm-owned persisted value by ordinary static inspection. The descriptor alone is insufficient to claim a new farm ledger or to prove the absence of one. Mark this as **opaque, no validated Dashboard reading** pending documented API/runtime evidence; do not count it as an accepted integration or an established missing farm-data adapter.

- Invoice VAT Account v1.0.0.0 has two byte-identical vault ZIPs (`FS25_373914_Invoice_VAT_Account.zip` and `KM_83070_Invoice_VAT_Account.zip`), SHA-256 `2BDD3B75299D20B68DA0C8FCA975C9BF0F898DEB02FCB46BF1DCC5D56232535E`. It depends on `FS25_Invoices`. Its only persisted state is a **save-wide** `recipientFarmId` setting in `invoiceVATAccount.xml`. On a paid invoice it credits that farm's native balance through `MoneyType.INVOICE_INCOME`; it keeps no VAT ledger, per-invoice payout history or mod-owned farm balance. A dashboard could label the configured recipient farm as a global setting, but must not present it as every farm's income or invent a VAT total from the setting. Native finance and the underlying Invoices repository are the appropriate sources for actual transactions. Classify as a settings-only, lower-priority capability unless a separate, source-backed farm reading is established.
- Farm Money Manager v1.0.0.0 has two byte-identical vault ZIPs (`ITCH_3652788_FS25_FarmMoneyManager.zip` and `ITCH_3837958_FS25_FarmMoneyManager.zip`), SHA-256 `F66CB0E22EA7DB15456D8DBFD56CFC16ABEC3FEF5C3F367ABEEDD5519D94CF4D`. Its two Lua files implement an admin-controlled amount entry/console command and a network event. The server validates a farm ID and calls native `g_currentMission:addMoney(amount, farmId, MoneyType.OTHER, ...)`, then prints old/new native balances. It saves no separate ledger, balance, budget or account state. The existing native farm finance reading already covers the balance; detecting this mod as an available money-editing control is honest, but a new Farm Dashboard data page would duplicate native state or invent transaction history. Count the two paths as one capability and classify it as no distinct mod-owned farm reading.
- Invoices - Production Sales v1.0.0.0 has two byte-identical vault ZIPs (`FS25_373258_Invoices_-_Production_Sales.zip` and `KM_82919_Invoices_-_Production_Sales.zip`), SHA-256 `791CECBDD7EF15B6C802DF012EDF3449DD15A941BB8DE9049D564DDC6F88FBDA`. It declares the `FS25_Invoices` dependency (version 1.2 or later). On the server, `g_currentMission.invoicesProductionSalesManager` has `offers`, `openBatches` and `closedReceipts`, with explicit seller/buyer farm IDs; offers and batches are persisted in its own XML. Its `buildSnapshot(farmId)` selects owned production outputs, buyer/seller batches and receipts for a requesting farm, but calls `rebuildProductionIndex` and `validateOffers`, so an exporter should read/filter the underlying state directly without invoking that builder. A farm-owned active product offer with a configured price/remaining quantity would add distinct value; a completed sale needs a second farm and the Invoices dependency and remains a separate gameplay test. Do not show the all-farm market as the current farm's sales or assume a single-farm save proves a transfer.
- Farm Limit Manager v1.0.0.0 has two byte-identical vault ZIPs (`FS25_372370_Farm_Limit_Manager.zip` and `KM_82655_Farm_Limit_Manager.zip`), SHA-256 `58C1005A23CB0F9DB6180DFA0C30877A244ADF115DBFBF6B689307A54E1D24B8`. `g_farmLimitManager.settings` holds five **save-wide** caps (active AI workers, husbandries, greenhouses, factories, contracts), defaulting to one each and persisted in `farmLimitManager.xml`; these are not per-farm settings. Its count helpers take a farm ID for active jobs/contracts and owned placeables. A dashboard could show the save-wide caps beside exact export-farm utilization, clearly distinguishing scope, but native counts alone are not a mod-owned capability. The source has no historical limit-hit or denied-purchase ledger. Classify as lower-priority settings/utilization candidate; a nondefault cap and a real blocked action would be needed to establish behavior beyond static defaults.
- Bale Batch Tracker builds `BaleBatchTracker.snapshot` every 5 seconds (every 1 second with fermenting bales). Its two vault paths (`FS25_374533_Bale_Batch_Tracker.zip` and `KM_83075_Bale_Batch_Tracker.zip`) have identical SHA-256 `C614FF07E8924D9666A731A12DD07B8F7FDEE9DA36C643275C171B16722B3EE6` and are one package. The snapshot includes physical/stored counts and litres, fermentation groups, ready counts and storage rows. Its source takes `g_currentMission:getFarmId()` as the snapshot farm. A collector must require `snapshot.farmId == exportFarmId`; if the mod has no farm ID on a dedicated server, it must report unavailable rather than export an all-farms aggregate. The normal dashboard already has a bale inventory, so the extra value is the mod's grouped fermentation and readiness view, not duplicate totals.
  A second read of the exact Lua confirms this snapshot is rebuilt live by `update`/`refresh`, not a persisted historical ledger. When `getFarmId()` is absent or resolves to spectator, `activeFarmId` is nil and both the loose-bale and object-storage scans deliberately accept every farm. The snapshot also contains live bale-object keys in `fermentingBales` that must never be serialized into Farm Dashboard. The **existing** collector requires exact snapshot/export farm ID and copies capped scalar groups; V4 already passed one bounded nonempty game-panel/export/installed-page reading. Headless/spectator data, an in-progress fermentation change and foreign-farm isolation in game remain unaccepted.
- Net Worth Tracker records `g_nwt_historyManager.histories` on `DAY_CHANGED` and persists those samples. The exact `NWT_historyManager.lua` has one category (`total`) and calls `getFarmByUserId(g_currentMission.playerUserId).farmId` without a nil guard, so it records only the local player's farm on that event; it does not iterate all farms. Dedicated-server behavior is unproved and may be unavailable. A read-only collector/page now reads the mod-local manager, filters `total` samples by the exact export farm ID, caps rows, and shows dated amounts separately from Farm Dashboard's current net-worth estimate. It does not call `recordFarmValue`, manufacture historical points or imply that an absent row means zero worth. A focused Lua/merge test proves nonempty farm isolation and rejects a shared-global shadow; UI typecheck and 27-locale key checks passed. The mod's value calculation includes money, debt, equipment, land, placeables, livestock and stored fill, which may differ from Farm Dashboard's current estimate. V6 established one nonempty single-farm amount reading; other farms and day changes remain untested.

- Frozen `TEST-PACKET-V6.md` SHA-256 `2C1FA03206E8E61ADE3817769404CA6A2CA2E2DF953CABECDAC497D021F3EF53` ran as `FARMHUB-ALLY-20261101-VAULT-V6` on a disposable unsaved save. The game history was empty before the day rollover and gained one row, `August 1, 2026 | 13,241,476 GBP`, afterward. Active slot a, generation 12, exported one farm-1 `total` row `{dayId:19, year:2, period:7, dayInPeriod:1, amount:13241476.143938}`; slot b, generation 11, remained empty. The installed 5.2.30 full page showed one recorded day and `13,241,476` for game day 19, year/period/day `2/7/1`. I independently rehashed the exact packet, installer, exporter and third-party ZIP, decoded both export slots, checked their payload byte lengths and Adler-32 fields, inspected the game/page screenshots and log, and read the full Ally restoration report. The amount agrees to the nearest pound across game, export and page; the before/after state proves a day-change capture. The game renders `August 1` while the export/page use numeric period `7`. The exact third-party GUI calls `g_i18n:formatPeriod(((periodId-1) % 12), false)` while its history writer copies `environment.currentPeriod`; the engine's period-to-month mapping was not independently established here, so do not claim a date-label defect or exact date-label three-way agreement. Tina reported one Windows row, 12 generations, StockGuard READY 79 and complete baseline-hash restoration of game/profile/mods/settings/app/shortcuts. The only rollover Lua stack was in `FS25_RedTape` (`TaxStatementPaidEvent.lua:38`), not Net Worth Tracker or Farm Dashboard. Hide-on-uncheck, multiple samples, other farms and dedicated-server behavior remain untested. This is a bounded nonempty amount-reading pass, not vault-wide acceptance or a public release.
- The gameplay `FS25_339475_FS25_netWorthTracker.zip` and uncategorized `KM_72515_Net_Worth_Tracker.zip` are byte-identical v1.1.0.3 archives, SHA-256 `00A27FC7A8A9A239DC19DE411AF89B28B9F9EE385DCA28D577D4FDE100B974E9`. - SWU Net Worth Tracker v1.0.0.1 (`ITCH_4632292_FS25_SWUNetWorthTracker.zip`, SHA-256 `408C3048ADC749AF9819CD4BF9E5F3E49639AF4DE57F2BA9CF33F1EF4BF4BACF`) is a separate implementation. The history rows have **no farm ID**; the calculation chooses a farm through the local player, user ID 1, then a fallback to the first found farm. Reading this global as the export farm would be unsafe in multiplayer and may be wrong even in a multi-farm save. - Productions Manager keeps `ProductionScheduleManager.data[pointKey][productionId] = { months, scheduleEnabled }` and enforces the schedule on period change. The source actually loads/saves this table at the **profile-wide** `modSettings/ProductionSettings.xml`, despite its comment calling it savegame XML; entries contain no save or farm ID. A safe reader must resolve each stored key and production ID to a *currently existing* production point and line owned by the export farm (the source prefers owning-placeable unique ID, then numeric ID aliases), omit unresolved/orphan entries, and never call `rebuildRuntimeMap`, `applyAll`, or `commitFromUI`. Even a live key match cannot prove that a profile-wide entry was originally configured in this save, so installed acceptance must disclose that identity limit and use a newly set, named nondefault schedule on a disposable save. The scheduled months are distinct from a line's current on/off state. Empty/default schedules are detection only, and a period rollover would be needed to prove enforcement.
- Market Price Helper's actual runtime object is `g_priceTicker`. It persists sampled per-fill-type prices with day/hour/period in `history` and personal alarm choices. The exact `PriceTicker:getConfigFilename()` selects savegame `priceTicker.xml` for a local client but profile-wide `modSettings/FS25_PriceTicker/priceTicker.xml` for a remote multiplayer client; neither record carries a farm ID. The source creates the instance only when `g_client` exists, so a headless dedicated server can have the mod loaded but no readable history object. Its stock routine returns all farms if no current farm ID is available. The `KM_79555_Market_Price_Helper.zip` and `FS25_362332_Market_Price_Helper.zip` vault paths are byte-identical (SHA-256 above), so count one package. A future reading would need explicit local-client and save/farm identity, a nondefault historical sample or alarm, and an unavailable state on a headless server. Native current sell prices, all-farm stock and an unscoped remote-client profile must not be presented as new farm-owned data. No collector or installed acceptance exists.

- Storage Alert Monitor v1.0.1.3 (`KM_82912_Storage_Alert_Monitor.zip`, SHA-256 `635AC44D6B46822F64B224B16BDB9453FEE7AC3987E19D2E5543E4E52E89C890`) is a client-only alert overlay, not a second storage inventory. `StorageAlertMonitor.rules` contains user-chosen low/high thresholds keyed to a placeable and product; `refreshAlerts` compares them with native live fill levels and capacities. Rules persist under profile `modSettings/StorageAlertMonitor/<save-hash>_<farmId>.xml`, while `update` returns on a dedicated server. Its save hash uses savegameDirectory or map ID fallback, so exact save identity and current farm must be proven before reading it. Nondefault threshold plus a triggered owned-building alert could be meaningful, but a headless exporter cannot assume the client object exists or claim current stock as mod-owned state. Classify as client/profile-scoped candidate, not yet integrated or installed-tested.
- Helper Profiles v2.1.1.0 (`GH_SimGamerJen__FS25_HelperProfiles_FS25_HelperProfiles.zip`, SHA-256 `1C03482C1C35EDD338CA387341E658C3212566BDC59B3CEC8A9D251B6B23EBEC`) is explicitly single-player only. It expands the standard helper identities to 20 slots, lets the player choose roster availability and preferred/first-free hiring, and can bind appearances; optional HelperPayroll owns pay roles and wages. Its availability file is under local profile `modSettings/FS25_HelperProfiles/saves/<savegameName>/roster.xml`, keyed by save slot and helper identity rather than farm ID, with `unknownSavegame` fallback. The live integration API reports selected helper, enabled counts and pick mode but no farm identifier. This is useful local player preference state, not a safe farm-scoped reading from a dedicated-server exporter; helper activity or wages must not be inferred from the preference file. A future local-only reading would need exact save/profile identity, the compatibility guard's active state and nondefault roster selection in game. No collector, installed comparison or multiplayer claim is made.
- Statistics Extended v1.0.0.1 has two byte-identical vault ZIPs (`FS25_364343_FS25_statsExtended.zip` and `KM_79084_Statistics_Extended.zip`), SHA-256 `EE6C655FE2E61D2DC0A19B14884AC316C802F106C1729D85A31095BA2450643C`; count them as one capability. Its added in-game Statistics tab formats `farm.stats.statistics` session/total values and derives achievement progress from `farm.stats`, current owned vehicles/placeables and the game achievement/collectible systems. The five Lua sources contain no mod-owned persistence or farm-specific result register. A Farm Dashboard native statistics page could reproduce selected useful measures with exact farm filtering, but that would be an enhancement to native data, not a new companion ledger. Some achievement data is map/player-wide, so it must not be assigned to the export farm by title alone. No separate collector or installed companion acceptance is justified from this mod's source.
- Farmland Auctions v1.0.0.2 (`KM_76821_Farmland_Auctions.zip`, SHA-256 `C2487DBF2627C982FA21030008403703C6EB53329D7C83C7DD5D845DE6199A6E`) holds one **shared current auction** in `AuctionManager:getCurrentAuction()` and saves it to save-local `auction.xml`. Its current highest bidder has a `currentHighestFarmId`; `bidHistory` entries have `farmId`, bid price and time, but `_appendHistory` retains only the latest four bids. That is distinct from native farmland ownership and price while an auction is active. A farm-scoped reader could show a participation/leading state only when the exact export farm is the current highest bidder or appears in those recent entries, with shared auction details clearly labelled as market context. Absence from the four-entry history is not proof that the farm never bid. The manager drops the auction after completion/cancellation; the source provides no completed-auction or paid-price ledger to export as a farm's purchase history. A nonempty bid, subsequent outbid/win and game/export/installed comparison are untested; do not interpret a scheduled auction or global `hadPlayerBid` as this farm's bid.
- Rainwater For Greenhouses v1.0.0.0 (`KM_83404_Rainwater_for_Greenhouses.zip`, SHA-256 `BD46E7312B48BBF010255943CD1E849D558F032C4104E4A30BE15C580A91A448`) adds collected rain directly to eligible farm-owned greenhouse/orchard native production WATER storage. `KotRainWater:collect` uses the current storage fill level/free capacity and writes through `storage:setFillLevel`; that water is already part of the normal production save and Farm Dashboard's native storage reading. The mod's settings live in profile `modSettings/FS25_KotRainWater.xml`; its collected-litres counters and per-placeable rain-depth records are session-only, not a saved per-farm rainwater ledger. The console status reports session totals and current native water. A separate page based on current water would duplicate native data, while a historical rainwater total would be invented. Classify as native-data overlap plus profile-wide settings; no distinct persistent farm reading or installed comparison is established.
- Configurable Animal Reproduction v1.0.0.1 (`KM_82013_configurable-animal-reproduction.zip`, SHA-256 `03651783F9D01FC028458A0ED2291612A991ECB00FD9486C1D851B99E053E6C7`) changes shared animal-subtype reproduction duration and production factors alongside Enhanced Animal System. - Implement Usage Counter v1.2.0.0 adds seven specialisations and persists per-implement values in the vehicle save XML. Its six area counters attach `usageCounter` and `sessionCounter` to the **existing native** `spec_cultivator`, `spec_cutter`, `spec_mower`, `spec_plow`, `spec_sowingMachine` and `spec_sprayer` tables, measured in hectares. A reader must first verify this exact mod is loaded; the mere existence of a native spec table does not prove a counter is installed. Bale wrapping uses the mod-qualified `spec_<loadedModName>.baleWrapCounter` table with `lifetimeCounter` and `sessionCounter` measured in **bale counts**, not hectares. Read only currently owned vehicles with supported specialisations, preserve the resettable-session meaning, and keep each unit family separate. Native operating hours do not duplicate these counters. A zero-initialised fresh vehicle proves only the reading path; nonzero fieldwork or bale-wrap gameplay is still needed for acceptance.
- Vehicle Inspection (HU) v1.0.0.0 has two vault paths (`FS25_373915_Vehicle_Inspection_HU.zip` and `KM_82788_Vehicle_Inspection_HU_.zip`) with identical SHA-256 `30CF475C88045BD6BFC4B48167A9C780D4F764328DD9BC3FA3CD096975EB558C`; count them as one package. Its `g_huManager.records` is keyed by vehicle unique ID. `ensureVehicleRecord` assigns `farmId` from the currently owned vehicle and stores class, interval, custom interval and remaining months; `getStatus` distinguishes valid/due/overdue; the monthly update decrements remaining months. The mod saves `huSystem.xml` and synchronises state to clients. A read-only dashboard reading can filter exact `record.farmId == exportFarmId` and join to currently owned vehicle IDs, excluding stale or foreign records; due counts and a named vehicle countdown are distinct from native vehicle maintenance. The mod's first scan grants full intervals, so a newly installed default state would prove data flow only, not the due/overdue transition or inspection action. Verify a nonempty owned record and later period behavior before calling gameplay acceptance.

- Finance Your Fleet v1.0.0.1 (`KM_81456_Finance_Your_Fleet.zip`, SHA-256 `B7E2E1C27A99C7C9D8651438420435EADA481BF2B8225AFBBC7458F77AB3DE8B`) is a separate equipment-finance lead. The live server `g_currentMission.financeManager.financeAgreements` and save-local `financeAgreements.xml` retain explicit `farmId`, vehicle unique ID, deposit, term/final payment, payments made, late count and recovery state; a durable profile mirror exists for save-directory replacement recovery. A new read-only collector/page now requires the mod to be loaded and the live manager to be ready; it selects exact export-farm agreements, joins the stored `agreement.vehicle` to `vehicle.uniqueId` in the current owned-vehicle list, and withholds missing/foreign links. It never scans the cross-save mirror or `pendingMigrationLinks`, nor calls the mod's `getVehicle()` or `getUniqueId()` methods that may assign an ID. The mod's read-only scheduled-due and payoff calculations are displayed as current amounts/estimates, separate from completed payments. Focused Lua/merge tests prove a nonempty linked row, foreign-farm/foreign-vehicle/sold-vehicle exclusion and unready-register withholding; typecheck and 27-locale key checks passed. This is **source-tested only**: no private package, nonempty installed agreement or game/export/page comparison yet. The mod disables itself if legacy `LeasingOptions`/`LeaseDeal` classes or `FS25_HirePurchasingV2` are active, so compatible test selection is a prerequisite. This is one distinct finance capability, not a second copy of native vehicle operating cost.

- Market Price Helper v1.0.0.1 appears twice in the vault as `KM_79555_Market_Price_Helper.zip` and `FS25_362332_Market_Price_Helper.zip`; both paths have identical SHA-256 `FFB6930723D1EB9A8AC4712ECF03F9D68A072EA827593EF3471CBB0EC52CDFBE` and count as one package. Exact source review shows `PriceTicker:onMissionLoaded` exits without initializing prices, UI or preferences when there is no client. On clients, `updatePrices` reads the game's synchronized selling-station prices, while `enrichPriceData` combines those prices with currently owned stock to derive quality, revenue and recommendations. The mod's genuinely retained additions are a per-client HUD/alarm configuration and bounded price history; remote clients save these under their own profile `modSettings/FS25_PriceTicker/priceTicker.xml`, and local clients use save-local `priceTicker.xml`. Neither has an export-farm key, and the history belongs to the observing client rather than the farm. For the current farm-scoped server dashboard, this is native price/stock overlap plus personal client data, **not a missing farm-owned collector**. A separate opt-in personal history feature would need an explicit client identity and freshness contract; no installed comparison is claimed.

- Multi Production Monitor v1.0.0.0 (`KM_81445_multi-production-monitor.zip`, SHA-256 `A88031B6442BD0EC96A8BB494B0C68B47C59CD0BD37C2CAB7CB916AA4CE5F9FE`) builds its production/husbandry/storage status snapshot from current native game objects; its `buildSnapshot(farmId)` uses the production chain manager's farm-specific points. The save-local `FS25_MultiProductionMonitor.xml` persists global alert thresholds, `seenLines` and ignored monitoring keys, with no farm ID on those settings; HUD layout lives in profile-wide mod settings. Its status/counts substantially overlap Farm Dashboard's native production and animal readings. A new farm-owned page is not justified from the default HUD or native snapshot alone, and the save-wide alert preferences must not be labelled as one farm's settings in multiplayer. This is a source-reviewed native-overlap/settings lead; a nondefault alert and exact ownership proof would be needed to revisit it.

- Storage Dialog Plus v1.1.0.0 (`FS25_352682_FS25_StorageDialogPlus.zip`, SHA-256 `43D001800A77EC460A6DE5C8FCF765E0033119EA3214730D0DC334D2553FE949`) replaces the native object-storage dialog with a scrollable, farm-filtered list and guards multiplayer unload requests against stale selections. Its `sdpRevision` is an in-memory/network revision of native `spec_objectStorage.objectInfos`, not a saved stock ledger; the two Lua files have no mod-owned savegame writer. Farm Dashboard already reads object-storage inventory. Classify this exact package as UI/transaction safety plus native storage overlap, not a missing farm-owned data adapter. A gameplay acceptance of the dialog/unload guard would be a separate test.

- Better Bale Physics v1.0.0.2 (`FS25_372285_Better_Bale_Physics.zip`, SHA-256 `07795DC244809BEB9A4FBB3BBFF5146850C7A992AE017EA86898B79CDD8DB962`) is a server-side bale settling/freeze controller. Its `BalePhysicsController.dataByBale` tracks transient ACTIVE/SETTLING/STABLE/DISTURBED physics states and damping per live bale; `BetterBalePhysics` wraps bale load, mount, unmount and delete methods. The inspected Lua has no savegame writer, farm-keyed outcome or durable bale count. A transient frozen flag is not bale inventory or stored-bale progress, and Farm Dashboard already has native bale inventory. Classify this exact package as gameplay behavior with no distinct farm-owned dashboard reading; an in-game physics acceptance would be separate from this data audit.

- Production Direct Sell v1.0.0.1 (`GH_iNotrez__FS25_ProductionDirectSell_FS25_ProductionDirectSell.zip`, SHA-256 `08B5E01F3569C7361EDD0F58CDC660C58CC61D3292ABD87D874DA8941B82808A`) is a manual action on an owned production. It quotes live native selling-station prices and a one-time random delivery fee, then the server lowers native production stock and credits native `MoneyType.SOLD_PRODUCTS`. Its five Lua files and descriptor show no saved offer, unpaid receivable or mod-owned transaction history. Current quote/selection exists only in the dialog/session; native stock and finance are the durable result. Classify this exact version as action/UI plus native-data overlap, not another payment ledger. No sale gameplay or fee reconciliation is claimed from this source review.

- Auto Sell Silo v1.6.0.0 (`KM_83453_Auto_Sell_Silo.zip`, SHA-256 `2890AF2241FDDFB263EB3D2237AFE0D2F7E1FAC992F30AA93F1344F78D8F727F`) has a distinct **unpaid receivable** state. When the host sells farm-owned silo stock with a deferred payment option, the mod reverses the selling-station gross credit and appends `{farmId, amount, months}` to `AutoSellSilo.pending`; `onPeriodChanged` pays due entries through native money and removes them. `savePending` writes the farm-keyed outstanding entries to save-local `autoSellSilo.xml` on a game save. The save also holds one global selected pay-mode index. A read-only collector could filter pending entries by exact export-farm ID, show amount and remaining months, and label them unpaid, never as realized revenue. It cannot recover crop, station, original litres, sale time or paid history from those entries. The menu is host/server-only. A read-only exact-farm collector, array merge handler and dedicated page now show only unpaid amount/months from the live server register; source tests cover two owned rows, foreign-farm exclusion, malformed terms, client withholding and shadow-global rejection. Typecheck, Vite build and 27-locale fallback checks pass. V15 provides a bounded nonempty deferred-sale acceptance; month rollover and payout remain untested. The default empty register alone is detection, not meaningful-value acceptance.
  A private V15 candidate packages V5 5.2.40 installer SHA-256 `ADC4CE337D123B03949E2BB39ABCF87F4412AE89F8CEAF0ABFFEED5DD8E29C6B` and exporter 5.1.0.33 ZIP SHA-256 `A2FB73DE87DED57F326D116A25EB7FCE039AAECD16B3C103C83BE9FA33950DC6` under `Z:\Graham\agent-comms\farmhub-companion-acceptance\auto-sell-silo-candidate\`; the byte-identical valid-name `FS25_AutoSellSilo.zip` is SHA-256 `2890AF2241FDDFB263EB3D2237AFE0D2F7E1FAC992F30AA93F1344F78D8F727F`. Focused tests, typecheck, Vite build, 27-locale verification, ZIP archive checks and private NSIS packaging passed; the live update feed was preserved. After RF-1468 returned substantive PARTIAL and reported full Ally restoration, `FARMHUB-ALLY-20261101-VAULT-V15` was queued with frozen `TEST-PACKET-V15.md` SHA-256 `D981F9B0B6D0C51AC0ABD23A64D8522E5A7931E60DC6798C5A6AC92A49262A4D`. It caps one unsaved deferred sale at 100 L and stops on StockGuard refusal or unclear input. V14's export showed Wheat in an owned silo but is only a test lead. Tina returned a substantive V15 result and I independently reviewed the frozen hashes, game/page screenshots, XML envelopes and final exports. On the disposable unsaved farm-1 save, one 100 L Wheat sale with `IN 1 MONTH (+10%)` reduced stored Wheat from 120,304 to 120,204 L. The game showed GBP 167 waiting and an unpaid toast; both final committed slots carried one farm-1 pending row for GBP 167.85 with one month remaining, and installed V5 showed one deferred payment and GBP 167 owed. The game balance stayed unchanged. XML payload byte counts, Adler-32 checksums, route/slot publication sequences and decoded JSON matched; one Windows row, one target load each, StockGuard READY, zero Lua tracebacks/read errors and full as-found Ally restoration were evidenced. The older V7R1 Desktop-original shortcut exception remains open. This passes only the unpaid receivable reading. No game save, month rollover, payout, sale history, multiplayer or other combination was tested.

- Dynamic Market descriptor v1.3.0.0 (`KM_79085_Dynamic_Market.zip`, SHA-256 `D7422FE3552F9D0605B743D2ABE2DA316FCAC861129AA91E3BDB396BA83A1FBF`) changes **shared selling-station prices** and retains per-station/per-fill-type sales pressure (`percent`, overflow litres, peak and recovery milestone) in savegame `gameSettings.dynamicMarket.stationPressure`. It also saves five global market modes/thresholds and favorite fill types under `gameSettings`; the main Lua version constant still says 1.2.9.0, so preserve the descriptor/runtime discrepancy when testing. None of these saved rows has a farm ID. Current owned stock is used to compose client notices and stock-value views, but that does not make global pressure or preferences farm-owned. This is **shared-market context**, distinct from realized farm revenue: the existing read-only page exposes pressure and group settings, but a station-pressure claim still needs a nonzero game/export/page comparison, stable station/fill-type identity and an explicit save-wide scope label. The game's current selling price already reflects the mod's adjustment and should not be counted again as a new farm ledger. Farm Dashboard already has a `DynamicMarketCollector.lua`, merge handler and full page: historical D1 acceptance compared seven group trends/curves and in-game Market News percentages with committed export and installed V5 5.2.18. That is bounded market-context value, not a new missing integration. A nonzero station-pressure entry, changed global mode, sale and payout were not covered by D1; their installed behavior remains untested. The current collector exports shared state under a companion page, so its UI must avoid implying those rows belong uniquely to the selected farm.

- Production Info Hud v1.3.0.0 (`FS25_313960_FS25_ProductionInfoHud.zip`, SHA-256 `88845CD8BD5728C6518A12146909A36EB89FD507DB02CCCE78DF98C8319FEE6B`) reads current farm-owned production points, factories, husbandries and storages and simulates when inputs will run out or outputs will fill. Its `productionInfoHud.lua` selects `getProductionPointsForFarmId(farmId)` and `getFactoriesForFarmId(farmId)` and checks storage ownership. The forecast is derived from native fill levels, capacities, active recipes and rates; it is an estimate that can vary with weather, random recipes and production speed, as the mod description itself warns. The main Lua has no save/load XML path; the ZIP's XML writes are in the bundled HUD/display framework and display configuration. Thus this exact version offers a potentially useful **derived forecast**, but no distinct persistent farm-owned event, balance or setting to collect. Farm Dashboard already reads native production and storage state. Do not count the mod as a missing data adapter or claim the forecast matches the game without a named production, same-time simulation comparison and explicit uncertainty label. No collector or installed acceptance exists for this forecast.

- Snow Contracts v1.1.0.0 (`FS25_354811_FS25_SnowContracts.zip`, SHA-256 `47C58071387FA643895E09980BD1CC553AC382DBF02F6DB9CBA457433790CDCD`) is a distinct farm-scoped candidate despite its name: it pays a vehicle owner's farm for cleared snow on land the farm does not own. The server keeps `_pending[farmId]`, writes positive pending amounts to save-local `SnowContracts.xml`, and pays them via `addMoney` on a day change; `_paidTotal` is session-only and is not persisted. An honest reading could show this farm's unpaid snow-clearing amount, separate from money already credited, by selecting only the exact farm key. The mod does not expose a conventional mission/contract register. A read-only exact-farm collector and full page now show the mod-owned unpaid amount with a clear unpaid label; focused farm-isolation tests, typecheck, Vite build and 27-locale key checks passed. Snowy nonzero pending state, payout transition and installed game/export/page proof remain untested. The uncategorized `KM_76170_Snow_Contracts.zip` is byte-identical (same SHA-256 and v1.1.0.0), so it is the same candidate rather than a second integration.

- Grass Contracts v1.0.0.0 has two byte-identical vault paths (`FS25_377438_Grass_Contracts.zip` and `KM_83139_Grass_Contracts.zip`), SHA-256 `9300D32BC36D7F0CDF0AA34651028583203B26475C142C0A096FD0ADC10A71D4`. Like Snow Contracts, its name does not imply a native mission register: it accumulates `_pending[farmId]` while an owned mower clears eligible grass on another owner's land and writes positive pending amounts to save-local `GrassContracts.xml`. On day change it credits the farm with `addMoney`; `_paidTotal` is session-only. A read-only exact-farm collector and full page now show the mod-owned **unpaid** amount, separate from credited income. Focused farm-isolation tests and UI typecheck passed. A real nonzero mowing state, payout transition, committed export and installed page have not been checked, so this is source-tested integration only.

- Production Pallet Manager v1.0.0.0 (`FS25_376956_Production_Pallet_Manager.zip`, SHA-256 `8B68756405F364C2FD906525407E0D8B04A56EFEC171646C7DC768FDF63B2B0F`) is a distinct configured-capacity candidate. `PalletManager` saves per-farm default pallet capacity and normal-weight choice, plus per-source/fill-type capacity overrides in save-local `palletManager.xml`; managed pallet capacity/original capacity are also saved on pallet vehicles. The overrides have stable source keys but no farm ID in their saved rows. The mod validates ownership when a rule is entered, yet a later ownership change means an exporter must rejoin each override to a currently owned live production/husbandry/beehive source before assigning it to a farm. A read-only collector and full page now show the exact farm default and normal-weight choice, plus overrides only when the mod's current source enumeration and independent ownership check both match the export farm. Opaque source keys and orphaned rules are withheld. The mod's allowed capacity set and `-1` disabled rule are preserved, without calling its setters. Focused farm-isolation, default-only, client-withholding and nonmutation tests, TypeScript, Vite build and 27-locale verification passed. The collector and page were first source-tested; V14 subsequently verified one nondefault farm-default capacity in game, committed export and the installed page. Managed pallet output and production remain untested.
  The exact ZIP's `PalletManagerDialog` adds a farm-default row and an output row for each currently discovered source. Its registered `PM_OPEN_MENU` action opens the dialog; clicking a row immediately cycles the default through 1,000/2,000/3,000/4,000/5,000/10,000 L or an output through inherited/disabled/allowed explicit values and calls the mod setter. A bounded acceptance can therefore choose one clearly named owned greenhouse output and click once from its recorded baseline, then compare the dialog, fresh export and installed page without spawning or selling a pallet. The actual bound key and row focus must be verified in game, and the test must remain unsaved with full Ally restoration.
  V14 `FARMHUB-ALLY-20261101-VAULT-V14` is a **bounded nondefault configuration pass**. Evidence: `Z:\Graham\agent-comms\ally-fh-vault-v14-evidence\RESULT-V14-text.md`, game screenshots `ui\v14-g10.png` and `ui\v14-g11.png`, installed page `ui\v14-v5-04.png`, committed XML/decoded JSON in `export\`, measured game log and restoration readback. The frozen packet SHA-256 `613D1DB714DDA682D51C8C1A4B1F796DBB1B8FA9FD5ECA7DEF458EAF4C8376D4`, private V5 5.2.39 installer `7B9D12A15F774D31215D1E73D5FDB7EECC17E02CB636EB88D2337E9509A2D6E5`, exporter 5.1.0.32 `908B376E721E524243C4CC75C575B70238DF905E590E29A611DB70A71A0CBFEE` and exact Pallet Manager ZIP `8B68756405F364C2FD906525407E0D8B04A56EFEC171646C7DC768FDF63B2B0F` were independently rehashed. One click changed farm 1's default output pallet capacity from 2,000 L to 3,000 L; the game dialog, export generations 9/10 in both final slots and installed page agreed. The XML payload lengths, Adler-32 values and decoded JSON matched on the checked baseline and final slots. StockGuard reached READY; the measured log had one load line for each target and no FarmDash or Pallet Manager Lua stack, though unrelated texture, console-command and harvest-mission errors were present. Tina reported one installed Windows row and full as-found Ally hash restoration; the supplied screenshots did not independently show the Windows inventory row. The default-only change produced no per-output override row. This proves the setting and its display, **not** a managed pallet in operation, spawned pallet, production, sale, delivery, multiplayer or other combinations. The previous source-tested-only statement above describes the state before V14.

- AI Farm Manager (Chat Bridge) v2.0.0.0 (`FS25_AiFarmChatBot.zip`, SHA-256 `57AC78CF1EACAE867772621ABA30389FFDDE299B17052168394B78D155A9B03C`) is an HTTP/chat bridge. - Realistic Farming - State Ledger v1.0.1.0 (`FS25_StateLedger.zip`, SHA-256 `24CF5384CF4DD8F3ECA8A00345E32FDDFB0C23B2B379CAAFC82F945E94FD6BBC`) is RF ecosystem infrastructure, not a standalone farm-stat module. - Dashboard Live v2.1.0.1 (`FS25_DashboardLive.zip`, SHA-256 `8A9B059D655FC0561353702F594E0B64350620770CF76BD7D3BF94DA58160FEA`) is the parent in-cab display specialization. Page/orientation are per-vehicle presentation preferences, while temperature/fill/damage overlap native vehicle readings. Classify this exact package as presentation/native overlap; detection is useful, but a separate Farm Dashboard data page would not add a distinct operational result. - Dashboard Live v2.1.0.1 has two byte-identical ZIP paths (`FS25_DashboardLive.zip`, `KM_69395_Dashboard_Live.zip`), SHA-256 `8A9B059D655FC0561353702F594E0B64350620770CF76BD7D3BF94DA58160FEA`. Those are vehicle-specific display state, not a farm-wide operating ledger. A bounded owned-vehicle reading might show a named equipped machine's simulated temperature or chosen display page if a game screenshot and same-generation export show value beyond existing vehicle telemetry. It must filter current vehicle ownership, require the specialization on that exact vehicle, and label the temperature as Dashboard Live simulation. No collector or installed comparison exists. The byte-identical second ZIP is not another integration.

- Whitey's Farm Staff has two distinct versions: v5.2.3.0 (`ITCH_4682588_FS25_WhiteysFarmStaff_5_2_3.zip`, SHA-256 `BFA1DE4556444BB2DAB5CF9B5D4D027C5E1275960AAC4735F5C3B6A422CE7DC9`) and v5.2.4.0 (`ITCH_5233907_FS25_WhiteysFarmStaff_5_2_4.zip`, SHA-256 `67B85604573B0BC43D6DF786AB9552FA717D8A2CB207D05BEBEA29C611A79B13`). - Realistic Farm Finance v1.2.0.0 (`KM_82429_Realistic_Farm_Finance.zip`, SHA-256 `A0825086FC2BD500F5443582A90C295AC3DF8D85AFF7F04F9C3D721325F80074`) is a large, separate finance system. Its server-side `RFF_FinanceRepository` loads and saves `realisticFarmFinance.xml` in the active save; `self.farms[farmId]` and farm-filtered methods cover mod loans, asset finance, applications, tenancies, insurance and period accounts. Those contract and obligation records are distinct from native cash and transaction history; a current balance or calculated valuation alone would duplicate native readings. The first read-only collector/full page uses `g_currentMission.realisticFarmFinance.repository` only when `loaded=true`, filters exact export-farm **active** loans and shows the recorded principal and arrears separately for TERM, MORTGAGE and BULLET. V23R1 subsequently passed one bounded nonempty game/export/installed-page comparison for a term loan in a reduced four-mod career; normal mod-set coexistence, loan payments and persistence remain untested.

## Contract Manager source lead

Contract Manager v1.26.0.0 (`GH_Dostlar-Studio__FS25_ContractManager_FS25_ContractManager.zip`, SHA-256 `DEA8F23B964992503CE42AADB05C1C2A05D42B2E8D277A7A6DC0BF3433FBBA1F`) has a separate save-local `FS25_ContractManager.xml`. Its server `ContractManagerRegistry` stores active contract metadata, completed/failed/cancelled/timed-out history, per-farm earnings and penalties, and per-farm quota counters. `peekFarmStats(farmId)` and `getHistory(farmId)` are read-only; `getFarmStats(farmId)` creates a missing row and must not be used by a dashboard reader. `ContractManagerPartnership.list` also holds mission-keyed, farm-keyed contribution records. This is distinct farm-owned outcome data; the mod's offer reward does not equal a recorded payout until `onMissionDismissed` fills `entry.payout`.

The first read-only Contract Manager collector/full page requires the active mod and server register, filters active/history/stats to the exact export farm, shows offered reward separately from a recorded payout, and withholds client-only `remoteHistory` because it may be incomplete. It does not call `getFarmStats` or mutate the registry. Focused Lua/merge tests prove farm isolation, no mutation, offer-versus-payout distinction, client withholding and rejection of a shared shadow register; catalog detection, TypeScript and Vite build passed. English fallback labels are in the translation source; the existing 27-language set is intact. V12 now proves one active row through game/export/installed page, while outcome history and its money remain untested. This ZIP is not equivalent to the separate Crop Contracts forward-sale mod.

Private V12 candidate is staged under `Z:\Graham\agent-comms\farmhub-companion-acceptance\contract-manager-candidate`: V5 5.2.37 installer SHA-256 `1C3C5CA55F0A9FA42AED5D8138570780197DB3C75A14BD51AC3A324F42673095`, exporter 5.1.0.30 ZIP SHA-256 `96477A9F59EADFDCCA1390DEB11EB031FB22C34BFB42732424064968A9847F7E`, and the byte-identical canonical-name Contract Manager ZIP SHA-256 `DEA8F23B964992503CE42AADB05C1C2A05D42B2E8D277A7A6DC0BF3433FBBA1F`. The exporter has 115 safe unique entries and matching descriptor/runtime versions. The V5 private packaging guards passed and preserved the existing latest-rf.yml feed. Frozen `TEST-PACKET-V12.md` SHA-256 `BDA524D70F5955422A605EC5A0F0271235A551011E00FEA44F7885745F4C3596` requests one unsaved, bounded farm-owned contract ledger check, with a GBP 500 cap on an optional clearly presented cancellation. These hashes are candidate identities, not installed acceptance.

Exact request `FARMHUB-ALLY-20261101-VAULT-V12` was appended once to `Z:\Graham\agent-comms\to-tina.md` after V11R1 restoration and a fresh RF-ownership check. Tina returned a substantive **partial** result: one farm-1 Deadwood contract offered GBP 1,125, with a matching active row on the mod's game screen, both final committed export slots (generations 9 and 10), and installed V5 5.2.37. The optional cancel was correctly skipped because the displayed five-tree penalty exceeded the GBP 500 cap. ## Production Booster System source lead

The exact vault `KM_82331_Production_Booster_System.zip` is v1.0.0.0, SHA-256 `58D1267C5396C483DC524F43EAE9818C21C7129864CAE3A989C255706F04214C`. Its `PBS_ProductionBoosterSystem.lua` saves per-production cumulative base, actual and extra output, output-by-fill-type totals, booster consumption, cost and active-time statistics on each production point. It also saves input priorities and manual booster-enable choices. These are distinct mod-tracked figures, beyond a native current fill level. The source updates the output counters when an owned production runs; they are not sales, deliveries or realized profit. Static XML booster configuration is not a player's saved choice.

A first read-only collector and full page now take only server statistics for the export farm's currently owned points and list nonzero output-by-fill-type base, actual and extra litres. They do not invoke the mod's `pbsEnsureStatistics` helper, which initialises/mutates tables, or its save and control functions. The collector caps points, lines, output types and rows and excludes foreign-farm points even if the production manager supplies them. It deliberately omits aggregate output across different fill types, costs, priority settings and booster consumption from this first view. Focused source-shape tests cover a nonempty owned line, foreign exclusion and client-only withholding. **This is source-tested only**: no game run, positive production, committed export or installed-page comparison exists. Production Booster System's coexistence with the normal RF-owned StockGuard set is unproved; the Production Control refusal does not establish this mod's outcome. A meaningful installed test needs a disposable owned production that runs with the exact mod, a nonzero recorded output and matching game/mod statistic, export and installed page.

## Dynamic Dealership and Advanced Seeding Control source leads

Dynamic Dealership v2.0.0.0 has two byte-identical vault paths, `gameplay/FS25_369544_Dynamic_Dealership.zip` and `uncategorized/KM_80444_Dynamic_Dealership.zip`, SHA-256 `392DBDC07ED5B5124F596984C88A711293FDFA2836E9DD59B8FB7D6A1DF19F76`. A customer order retains item XML filename, quantity, delivery day, farm ID, deposit already paid and remaining amount; the source separately handles financed orders. The current dealer inventory and incoming stock are shared market state, not farm-owned. This is a genuine **active-order** lead distinct from native owned vehicles or the separate Vehicle Trade In completed-trade history. A read-only collector and full page now take only the bound server manager's live CUSTOMER orders with an exact export-farm ID and resolved store-item name; they show the mod's order fields, with a clear warning that a financed order's remaining field is not its full loan balance. The reader calls no purchase, delivery or save functions. Focused source-shape tests cover nonempty farm filtering, shared-stock exclusion, foreign-farm exclusion and client withholding. Delivery, financing, saved persistence, multiple orders and multiplayer remain untested.

Its vehicle specialization saves mode, follow-up width, track width, current tramline lane and starter-fertilizer enable on each seeder in native `vehicles.xml`. A possible owned-vehicle page should prove a nondefault seeder setting and current vehicle ownership before claiming value. No reader, game work pass or same-generation installed comparison exists; two ZIP paths do not imply two missing integrations.

## Vehicle Trade In source lead

Vehicle Trade In v1.0.0.1 (`FS25_362333_FS25_TradeInMenu.zip`, SHA-256 `CD8F6BD2189328595536A4BAA2F62FB81F235611437134C310800DA41F01C310`) has a mod-owned `tradeInStatistics.xml` ledger. The exact `TradeInStatistics.lua` retains up to 200 completed dealer transactions, each with farm ID, vehicle name, base appraisal, final trade credit and date; `scripts/main.lua` attaches the runtime as `mission.tradeInMod`, and the mod's Statistics GUI reads that runtime. Completed trade credit is distinct from native current vehicle value. The used-market offer list is shared market state, and financing agreements have separate binding/payment rules; neither is copied by this first reader. A `main.lua` comment says 1.1.0.0, but the packaged `modDesc.xml` declares **1.0.0.1**.

The exact `serverFinalizeTrade` path records a history row only **after** a replacement-vehicle purchase succeeds and the old vehicle is removed. Starting a trade or viewing its appraisal does not create an accepted ledger row; cancelling refunds the temporary credit. A nonempty installed acceptance therefore needs one bounded, completed trade on a disposable unsaved save, with the original vehicle, replacement and credit clearly identified before confirmation. Do not infer completed history from a pending transaction or native vehicle sale.

A read-only collector and full page now take only the exact export farm's completed transaction rows from the server runtime and label the final value as trade credit, not profit. The mod's XML loader substitutes farm 1 for a legacy row with no farm ID before the dashboard can inspect its origin, so the reader withholds farm 1 history in multiplayer rather than risk misattribution. It also withholds client-only or unbound registers and never invokes the mod's mutating save/reset paths. Two focused tests cover nonempty farm isolation, base-appraisal versus final-credit values, no mutation and the multiplayer legacy-owner gate; TypeScript typecheck, Vite build and existing 27-locale verification passed. English fallback copy has 13 new labels. This is **source-tested only**. No completed game trade, same-generation export, installed page, finance payoff, or multiplayer acceptance exists. A future bounded test would require an explicitly approved disposable trade and exact before/after value comparison; an empty ledger alone would not prove value.

The exact `AS_Main.lua` constructs both paths from `getUserProfileAppPath()`, and `AS_XML.lua` reads and writes those profile files. These are player appearance preferences, not a save-local or farm-owned event, balance or operating metric. A per-farm Dashboard collector would misattribute the data; classify this exact version as profile customization rather than a missing farm-data adapter. No game-side appearance switching was tested.

Cultivator Plowing v1.1.0.1 (`KM_82552_Plowing_with_a_cultivator.zip`, SHA-256 `21015148FACBA5DFC28EBD1BEA8D8E19EE141DFD39CC5B2123FB78281E3E7A60`) adds a cultivator shop configuration for cultivation or plowing, injects native work modes where needed, and changes native field plow-level work. The exact specialization derives its selected mode from the vehicle's `configurations["cultivatorPlowMode"]`, defaults its runtime override to nil on load, and resets its limit-to-field switch on post-load; it only streams that switch to clients. The inspected Lua defines no separate saved operation count, completed-work ledger or farm-keyed state. A Dashboard vehicle page could display a currently owned implement's mode as a narrow configuration feature, but the resulting field condition is already native data and a transient runtime override should not be represented as durable progress. No nondefault owned implement or game/export/page comparison exists, so this is a lower-priority vehicle-setting lead, not an accepted meaningful-value integration.

Veterinarian and Animal Diseases uses `g_veterinarian.states` on the server. The exact mod's private husbandry key is `configFileName@x@z` with coordinates rounded to one decimal place; `veterinarian.xml` saves that key, the sick count, disease index and per-animal state without a farm ID. FarmHub's first reader recreates only the mod's key for live husbandries whose `getOwnerFarmId()` exactly matches the export farm. It exports named pen/animal and sick counts plus the disease category when sick, omitting animal names and treatment detail. Missing owned-pen state is marked partial; a client-only snapshot is withheld. Two focused Lua/merge tests, TypeScript typecheck, Vite build and existing locale verification passed. This is **source-tested only**: no positive illness, game treatment, nonempty installed page, multiplayer or save/load proof exists. A healthy zero in a pen is not a treatment pass.

This is a distinct order-progress lead but not yet safe to put on an arbitrary farm page. A single-player-only reader would need verified local/export farm identity and a real order with game/export/page comparison; no collector or installed pass exists.

- Mowing Contracts Plus v1.0.0.0 (`KM_82704_Mowing_Contracts_Plus.zip`, SHA-256 `90979F5DAB76489C1F165146582966C69DEF4D4E7C5C0144A0BCBCD9008D4E4A`) is a genuine narrow contract-state lead. On single-player server sessions it attaches `mcpChoice` (no offer/pending/accepted/declined) and `mcpReduction` to native `mowMission` objects and saves them under each mowing mission's `mowingContractsPlus` XML node. An accepted offer reduces that contract's reward (source default 50%) in exchange for continued grass-access work; the source's normal offer chance is 25%, with forced offers disabled. A safe reader requires the exact mod loaded, single-player server state, exact `mission.farmId == exportFarmId`, a mowing mission and a recorded choice. It must read the fields directly: `ensureOffer` can create a new choice and must never be called by an exporter. An offered or accepted decision would be distinct from native contract progress, but a game offer, fresh committed export and installed page have not been compared. A default/no-offer contract is detection only; do not claim reduced payment or retained access without completing the gameplay path.

  A first read-only collector and full page now implement that narrow reading. They require the mod's bound symbol, a single-player server mission register and exact assigned farm ID, show only pending/accepted/declined choices with the potential reward reduction, and exclude unrelated or unassigned contracts. They never call `ensureOffer` or report a payout or collected grass. A focused Lua/merge test covers a nonempty accepted and pending decision, foreign-farm exclusion, no offer mutation, multiplayer/client withholding and rejection of an unbound symbol; TypeScript typecheck, Vite build, catalog test and 27-locale verification passed. This remains **source-tested only**. A real assigned offer, game prompt/decision, committed export and installed page comparison are still required before meaningful-value acceptance; the normal 25% offer chance may make a bounded test inconclusive.

Its toggleable wrapper specialization persists `currentDropModeState` (manual, automatic or collect) and `baleWrapperIsActive` on the baler-wrapper vehicle. A separate drivable-host specialization persists `wrappedBaleCount` on the tractor or integrated self-propelled baler that controls it. That count is a short-lived collect-mode queue reset during wrapping/unloading; it is **not** a lifetime bale total or a property to attribute blindly to the attached baler. A read-only owned-vehicle reader could expose the baler-wrapper mode and toggle when this exact mod is loaded; it should include the host queue only when the current attachment relationship and ownership are proved. That would be distinct configuration value if a nondefault mode is used, but a default manual/active reading is detection only. No collector, nondefault game setting, fresh export or installed acceptance exists; native `wrappedBales` statistics must not be relabelled as this mod's count.

A separate opt-in server-admin configuration view would need role/privacy design and a named nondefault override comparison. No farm-scoped collector or installed acceptance exists.

## Market Dynamics RF-owned futures boundary

The exact `FS25_MarketDynamics.zip` v1.3.1.43 (SHA-256 `D59ADC02B073E399920F1748297B1667A5D6B65046E81DFAF658A99ABF774051`) is RF-owned. Its `MarketSerializer.lua` saves per-fill-type price history and futures contracts in the save; each contract carries a `farmId`, commodity, target/delivered litres, locked price and status. Farm Dashboard already has an RF Market Dynamics aggregate reader, so this is not a new missing-mod page. Source review found that its futures summary copied contracts from all farms and wrote a missing ID into the mod's contract table. Farm Dashboard source now withholds futures until the export farm is resolved, includes only exact-farm contracts and uses a local ID without modifying the RF contract. Focused Lua tests cover farm isolation, unresolved-farm withholding and no mutation. This fix was made after the private V11R1 5.1.0.29 ZIP was packaged, so that candidate does **not** contain it. No installed multi-farm futures acceptance exists, and RF source was not changed. Market-wide events and price movers remain save-wide context rather than farm-owned contract rows.

## Real Land Finance V11 installed evidence and due-date correction

V11 passed a bounded, unsaved Field 76 lease for farm 1. The game showed annual rent GBP 656 and a GBP 383 first debit; both final committed export slots held one owned lease row with raw annual rent 656.758, and installed V5 5.2.35 displayed the same contract and rent at the game's whole-currency precision. The empty baseline was distinctly reported as `settings-only/no-farm-contracts`. Tina reported one Windows row, committed generations, normal StockGuard READY, no relevant Lua stack and full restoration to the V11 as-found Ally hashes. The older V7R1 Desktop shortcut original bytes remain unrecovered.

V11 also exposed a FarmHub export defect: `monthsToNext=12` while the game showed the next lease rent due in 7 months. Real Land Finance's `FTRManager:getNextDue` calculates a yearly lease from the current calendar period; the collector had copied `contract.monthsToNext`. The collector now calls that read-only method, and a focused 12-to-7 test passes. Private exporter 5.1.0.29 and V5 5.2.36 are source versions awaiting an installed retest. Separately, the game's contract details showed total paid GBP 0 after the GBP 383 debit; that third-party display/state meaning is unresolved and was not exported as a FarmHub payment claim. No save-local mod XML was written because the disposable game was not saved. Payment rollover, arrears, other farms and multiplayer remain untested.

The focused V11R1 private retest is queued with Tina under `FARMHUB-ALLY-20261101-VAULT-V11R1`; ACK is receipt only. ## Farmland Marketplace v1.0.5.0 source correction
