- Diesel Fuel System v1.0.0.0 (`uncategorized/KM_82984_Diesel_Fuel_System.zip`, SHA-256 `FF520FC82B6DA050A5FD2CF73EF7781B8770C849D9B731AADD36B3B3214DBBC8`) is a distinct, source-backed maintenance and stored-fuel lead. The exact `SCVehicle.lua` attaches to diesel tractors and combines and saves per-vehicle fuel-lot condition, filter obstruction/hours, deposits, trapped water and air/priming state in `vehicles.xml`; `SCPlaceableTank.lua` saves fuel-lot, water, filter and metered-litres state on its placeable tank. `SCPrecio.lua` separately saves a daily diesel-price factor/history for the save, while the game diesel fill type already exposes the resulting current price. A read-only dashboard view could add useful **owned vehicle/tank condition** beyond native fuel litres, but it must select the current export farm's vehicles/placeables, avoid presenting transient calculated effects as completed service, and keep the save-wide price history separate from farm assets. The two Realistic Diesel Price vault ZIP paths are byte-identical SHA-256 `5B5A2FE766344A0CC9C3468E6F03116223AF724949E5C59D91FA7272957179D2` and belong to a separate already catalogued price-preset mod; they are not additional Diesel Fuel System copies. A first read-only owned-vehicle collector and full page now read the live `spec_<modName>.scFuel` or bare `spec_scFuel` table directly for applicable diesel vehicles owned by the export farm. They show the mod's filter hours and obstruction, trapped-water litres, fuel-lot age and oxidation, and explicit air/missing-filter indicators; they do not call the mod's `getSpec` or `scGetFilterState` helpers because those initialise missing state. A focused source-shape/merge test proves a nonzero owned tractor row, foreign/electric exclusion and client withholding; TypeScript typecheck passed. This first view omits the separate placeable tank and save-wide price history. It is **source-tested only**: no nonzero in-game condition, committed export, installed page, multiplayer or coexistence comparison exists, so this is not yet an accepted integration.

The full page shows the named recipe and its selected modes separately from output settings, without claiming ingredient use or production. Exact source shows the server cache is warmed once per field on update, while later cache refreshes depend on network requests; a cached entry may be stale. The mod's `getFarmIdForField` falls back to the current player farm when a farmland owner is unavailable, so a reader must independently verify the live farmland owner and never trust that fallback for farm isolation. The game list showed one contract, entry 45, margin 4522 and expiry 7; later committed export generations 10–13 and installed V5 5.2.48 page showed the same farm-1 crop, direction, amount, leverage, entry 45.23 and margin 4522.70 at whole-currency display precision. The export also recorded expiry index 7; the supplied installed-page crop is horizontally clipped before that value, so the expiry cell is not independently screenshot-proved. I independently rehashed the frozen packet (`68646BDB06AC174A6496A7EFA92129DF1FAA15BC2E878BDDA10A7DE0786B5608`), installer, exporter and exact mod ZIP; decoded all six after/after2/final XML envelopes with matching lengths, Adler-32 and JSON, viewed the offer/game/page screenshots, checked the one Windows row, active marker (slot b, generation 13, sequence 14), load/StockGuard logs and Tina's 01:53:48 full Ally hash restoration. Earlier committed generations 7–9 still reported zero positions after the order, even though the game showed one by 01:45; generation 10 first reported it around 01:48. The cause of this lag was not established, so a fresh page should not be assumed to reflect the order immediately. Crucially, the exact third-party source inserts the contract at `FuturesMarket.lua:215` then calls `NotificationType.INFO` at line 230; the measured run threw `attempt to index nil with 'INFO'` on that line just after order confirmation. That prevents the subsequent contract broadcast at line 234 from running in that call and left the wizard open. No FarmHub Lua stack was measured, but this mod error blocks a claim of healthy full-game or multiplayer behaviour. The game displayed unrealized P&L; the exporter/page intentionally omit it. No close, settlement, realized profit, price movement, margin call, saved persistence, multi-farm or multiplayer test was done; no public release follows.

- Vehicle Control Addon v9.0.0.26 (`uncategorized/KM_82841_Vehicle_Control_Addon_VCA_.zip`, SHA-256 `B5F868226A527A9CBC406FDC0438E56751075E2D6131CDED575D144653D9A73A`) is a **distinct source-backed current-settings lead**, not an acreage/work ledger. Its `lua/vehicleControlAddon.lua` registers `spec_vca` properties on vehicles; `onPostLoad` reads nondefault per-vehicle values from the vehicle XML and `saveToXMLFile` writes the saved properties. Live per-vehicle settings include steering mode, guidance angle/distance/offset, limiter and differential choices; some operating flags such as `snapIsOn`, `keepSpeed` and `pathRecording` are explicitly marked unsaved, so they must not be presented as durable history. A separate `vehicleControlAddon.xml` stores global/default preferences, which must not be mistaken for every owned vehicle's actual state. A farm-owned read-only view of current named-vehicle configuration could be useful beyond native speed/fuel values. At this initial source-review stage there was no collector or installed comparison; the later V31/V31R1 evidence below supplies a bounded setting transition, while other settings and multiplayer remain untested.

- Field To-Do List exact source check adds a stricter migration boundary to the earlier candidate note. `ToDoManager:loadFromXMLFile` calls `migrateOrphanTaskFarmIds()`, which assigns every legacy task missing `farmId` to the then-local farm in memory; `taskBelongsToFarm(task, farmId)` also returns true for a nil task farm ID. Therefore filtering the **live** `manualTasks` table by numeric `farmId` alone cannot prove historical ownership after load. A safe multi-farm reading needs original saved-row provenance (or a source migration contract that identifies legacy rows), plus current field ownership for planned crops; creating a normal manual task in a disposable save would test only new-task handling. The collector remains deferred instead of presenting potentially misattributed plans as another farm's work.

- PTO Load System v1.0.0.0 (`gameplay/FS25_372063_PTO_Load_System.zip`, SHA-256 `4AAEFA588D11BD2817E491F3FAF366A20C8DF534FB0F722D740931EDD44E64FF`) has a **distinct transient calculation lead**, not a saved power or work ledger. Its `PtoLoadModel.calculate` requires a running engine, traverses attached PTO consumers, combines current torque-derived power with configured minimum/harvest estimates, and calculates a requested-versus-available load percentage. `g_ptoLoadSystem.localState` is bound to the locally occupied vehicle and smooths that estimate for the HUD; server `serverStates` tracks overload/cooldown timers per root vehicle. Only HUD position is written to its `FS25_PtoLoadSystem.xml` settings file. A farm page would need a proven read-only server calculation for an owned, currently running PTO combination, and must label requested power/load as a model estimate while keeping actual consumed torque separate. Calling the model from an exporter and its dedicated-server/farm ownership behavior are not yet tested, so no collector or installed acceptance is claimed.

- Harvestmission Creator v1.0.0.0 (`gameplay/FS25_373445_Harvestmission_Creator.zip`, SHA-256 `54B4C9863E900F6AA7FBF060F51C818E7993EB80B74B75A6EF4396FF7BAB431D`) is a **game action and native-contract overlap**, not a separate saved mission ledger. Its source offers a dialog/console command, checks field and fruit eligibility, temporarily applies a harvest-ready field state, calls the game's harvest-mission generator, registers the resulting mission and refreshes the normal contracts list. If generation fails it restores the original field state. A successful mission is visible through native contract data already collected by Dashboard; the ZIP has no independent farm-keyed result/history table to display. This source classification does not prove that a generated contract or field restoration works correctly in game, and no gameplay test was run.

Its `lastWidth` describes the attached header geometry already available as machine context; a new Dashboard page for this one value would duplicate that context. This classification does not prove the in-game spread effect or combination with other work-area mods.

Its `PFOrganicCropDemandUnlock` keeps a target crop plus `TARGET_MINUS` or `MAX_APPLICATION` nitrogen adjustment in kg N/ha, keyed by the implement's unique ID in save-local `pfOrganicCropDemandSelector.xml` and synchronized during play. This is source-tested only: no nondefault installed game/export/page comparison, normal StockGuard/PF coexistence, applied nitrogen, yield or multiplayer proof exists yet.

- Follow-up on V39 Powertrain's absent `spec_ptuUpgrade.base`: the exact third-party source creates `spec_ptuUpgrade` in `Motorized.onLoad`, then attempts to capture the base in its patched `Motorized.onPostLoad` by sampling keyframe `time` values. The local FS25 `AnimCurve.lua` and `Motorized.lua` extract show that a normal torque curve has keyframes with `time`; V39 counted keyframes but did not count their `time` fields or prove that the Powertrain post-load wrapper executed. Ally's V39 log also says OutOfFuel patched `Motorized:onPostLoad` after mod loading. That is a compatibility lead to inspect, **not** evidence that OutOfFuel caused the missing base. No root cause, applied upgrade or safe workaround has been established, and no RF or third-party source was changed.

- NPC Favor - Living Neighborhood v1.2.7.108 (`uncategorized/FS25_NPCFavor.zip`, SHA-256 `7E1816668B0EF5AC185DABA3F1C90C6E4A9693FBAC8A5AD09CFA5FC6659B359A`) is an **RF-owned, already integrated** package, not a newly missing mod. Its current source puts generated jobs into `favorSystem.activeFavors` as `pending` with `ownerFarmIdPresent=false`; accepting one stamps an ordinary `ownerFarmId`, sets the presence flag and changes status to `active`. The existing Farm Dashboard `RfNpcFavorDataCollector` previously included unowned/pending rows in each farm's active list because a nil owner matched every farm, and it also accepted a legacy `farmId` fallback. Its read-only filter now requires an explicit owner-presence flag, the exact selected farm ID and `active` or `in_progress` status. NPC relationship scores are shared world state in the third-party source, so the collector now exports them once as `worldRelationships`, the page labels them shared across farms, and the farm-specific overview highlight is withheld.
